# Change log

One row per section of the source site, from `audit/change-control.json` (792 rows on
154 pages). Each decision was applied with `sr-plan --set` by the rule in `tools/ledger-decide.mjs` that matched the row; `audit/ledger-rules.json` records which rule decided which row.

## Legend

- **PRESERVE**: kept as-is; content and function unchanged
- **IMPROVE**: same content and meaning, better structure, hierarchy or design
- **REPLACE**: superseded by a better structure (reason required)
- **REMOVE**: deliberately dropped (reason required)
- **ADD**: new section that did not exist on the source

Tally: IMPROVE 736 · REPLACE 16 · PRESERVE 6 · REMOVE 22 · ADD 12.

## Decisions by rule

| rule | rows | decision | narrative slot | rebuilt as | why |

| home-opening | 1 | IMPROVE | value-proposition | B.4 Home hero + B.5 Intro card + B.6 Envision promo (s4) and services (s5) | First home row. sr-plan makes home rows from h2/h3 only, so this row (label: the services h2) also stands for everything above it, which has no row of its own: the hero (s1 photo row; s2 "Comprehensive Eye Care", a div.ecp-heading at source, with "Request Appointment"), the h1 "Your Eye Doctor in Fort Myers, Florida" with its paragraph (s3) and the Envision promo image (s4). Rebuilt per DESIGN-SPEC 3.16: the hero (the statement as a p, the primary CTA, the practice photo in a 4:3 frame, the light, the bank), the glass intro card holding the h1, the promo straddling the services band, then the services heading. The value proposition: who it serves, what it offers, the primary CTA. Span: s1, s2, s3, s4, s5. Copy verbatim. |

The page's own structured data is carried or removed by the rows below. |
| added:rm-jsonld-blogposting | 1 | IMPROVE | footer | A.1 Head, in this order | IMPROVE (audit/clone-removals.json elements[18], declared REPAIR): The post's own BlogPosting is carried into the page graph (src/lib/seo.mjs carryBlogPosting); every key and value is the source's except these repairs: mainEntityOfPage.@id (the platform pointed every post at the home page) becomes the page's canonical; HTML entities inside the headline / description strings are decoded (3 posts); datePublished and dateModified ("February 26, 2023") become ISO 8601 dates, the same date the page prints; the empty image [""] is omitted; publisher.logo.url (the platform CDN copy) becomes the logo file this build ships. The build fails if a headline is not the page h1, a date does not parse, datePublished differs from the printed post date, or the author / publisher is not the practice. Blog posts with no BlogPosting at source get none: /inmode-forma-and-lumecca-a-breakthrough-in-eye-care/, /top-causes-of-dry-eye-in-fort-myers/. (count: 19) |
| added:rm-jsonld-faqpage | 1 | REMOVE | footer | A.1 Head, in this order | REMOVE (audit/clone-removals.json elements[19], declared REMOVE): The FAQPage block lists 4 questions and answers that no page of the site prints (the visible FAQ accordion of /eye-care-services/dry-eye-disease-and-treatment/ asks 3 other questions with other answers; the copy on the glaucoma page also names the dry-eye page as its url). Structured data describes what the page shows, so it is removed rather than published as page content (per-page why: audit/seo-repairs.json). Open: docs/OPEN-DECISIONS.md B. (count: 2) |
| added:rm-jsonld-videoobject | 1 | REMOVE | footer | A.1 Head, in this order | REMOVE (audit/clone-removals.json elements[20], declared REMOVE): The VideoObject block (the eye-safety YouTube video) does not parse at source - a raw line break inside its description string (JSON.parse: "Bad control character in string literal") - so no search engine read it. It is removed, not repaired: repairing it would publish values the source never published validly (uploadDate "2004-6-10" is not an ISO 8601 date; thumbnailUrl is a YouTube animated-preview URL, i.ytimg.com/an_webp/... with du / sqp / rs query parameters). The 2 tag archives that carry it do not show the video. Open: docs/OPEN-DECISIONS.md B. (count: 3) |
| added:rm-eyeglass-guide-badge | 1 | REMOVE | benefits-solution | B.22 Long-form prose | REMOVE (audit/clone-removals.json elements[21], declared REMOVE): Platform tool badge (classified platform-ui-drop/platform-tool-logo): the EyeGlass Guide is an online tool of the platform; its icon link goes with the image. The page copy stays. Whether the tool survives the move is an open decision (docs/OPEN-DECISIONS.md). |

| / | #1 Our Eye Care Services | IMPROVE | value-proposition | B.4 Home hero + B.5 Intro card + B.6 Envision promo (s4) and services (s5) | First home row. sr-plan makes home rows from h2/h3 only, so this row (label: the services h2) also stands for everything above it, which has no row of its own: the hero (s1 photo row; s2 "Comprehensive Eye Care", a div.ecp-heading at source, with "Request Appointment"), the h1 "Your Eye Doctor in Fort Myers, Florida" with its paragraph (s3) and the Envision promo image (s4). Rebuilt per DESIGN-SPEC 3.16: the hero (the statement as a p, the primary CTA, the practice photo in a 4:3 frame, the light, the bank), the glass intro card holding the h1, the promo straddling the services band, then the services heading. The value proposition: who it serves, what it offers, the primary CTA. Span: s1, s2, s3, s4, s5. Copy verbatim. |

| / | #1018 clone-removals elements[18] REPAIR: source JSON-LD BlogPosting on 19 page(s) | IMPROVE | footer | A.1 Head, in this order | IMPROVE (audit/clone-removals.json elements[18], declared REPAIR): The post's own BlogPosting is carried into the page graph (src/lib/seo.mjs carryBlogPosting); every key and value is the source's except these repairs: mainEntityOfPage.@id (the platform pointed every post at the home page) becomes the page's canonical; HTML entities inside the headline / description strings are decoded (3 posts); datePublished and dateModified ("February 26, 2023") become ISO 8601 dates, the same date the page prints; the empty image [""] is omitted; publisher.logo.url (the platform CDN copy) becomes the logo file this build ships. The build fails if a headline is not the page h1, a date does not parse, datePublished differs from the printed post date, or the author / publisher is not the practice. Blog posts with no BlogPosting at source get none: /inmode-forma-and-lumecca-a-breakthrough-in-eye-care/, /top-causes-of-dry-eye-in-fort-myers/. (count: 19) |

| what | decision | reason |
|---|---|---|
| header.ecp-header, footer.ecp-footer, div.ecp-global-footer, div.ecp-secondary | REPLACE | The source chrome (top bar, header, 4 copies of the primary menu, footer, sidebar widgets) is rendered from src/content/chrome.json, whose every value tools/write-facts.mjs verifies against all chrome-bearing raw pages. |
| footer form#voice_search.ecp-voice-search ("Speak Field") | REMOVE | Voice search form (144 files): posts to WordPress /?s=, which a static build does not serve; its transcript script cannot even reach the field (SITE-ARCHITECTURE section 7). |
| div.ecp-secondary .widget_search, main .fl-module-ecp-search, the "Nothing Found" search prompts, the /sitemap/ search form | REMOVE | WordPress search forms (sidebar on 132 pages; 14 inside the content regions): no search backend in a static build. The sentence "Perhaps searching can help." on the four "Nothing Found" archives is source copy and stays. |
| div.ecp-global-footer a.ecp-powered-by (+ eyecarepro-logo.svg) | REMOVE | Vendor credit "Powered by" + the EyeCarePro logo of the former platform; the "© 2026" before it stays. |
| a#ecp-footer-login-link | REMOVE | Login link to the platform WordPress admin (riversidefamilyeyecare.ecpbuilder.com/wp-admin); no such backend exists in the rebuild. |
| a.ecp-menu-mobile-focus-trap ("Return to top of menu") | REMOVE | Focus-trap link of the platform's mobile menu (146 files); the scaffold menu needs none. |
| nav.ecp-menu copies of the SITE menus in the /template/* pages (header, footer and mobile-menu copies) | REPLACE | The six /template/* pages are the platform's global header/footer templates rendered as pages. A menu whose every item is a chrome.json menu label (primary menu with its children, footer menu, utility links) is a copy of the site menus, rendered from chrome.json. Any other menu is page content and is kept (the phone menu of /template/header-2/, next row). |
| /template/header-2/ phone menu (div.ecp-menu-wrapper.ecp-menu-convert-at-desktop): its second, hamburger copy (nav.ecp-menu-hamburger-content) of the same items | REMOVE | The platform prints a menu twice, a horizontal nav and its hamburger copy; this one shows at every width only as the hamburger labelled "Call Us". Its label and its 3 items print once, as a list: "Call Fort Myers 239-500-2020" (tel:239-500-2020) and two "Call" items whose href is an empty tel: (unwrapped to the word, as everywhere: a tel: with no number dials nothing). Its focus-trap link is the "Return to top of menu" row. |
| GTM-P6GSK34 (script + noscript iframe), Google Analytics G-6LTG771EHQ, Google Ads conversion_async.js, Microsoft Clarity | REMOVE | Trackers of the current accounts: the rebuild must not report its visitors to them; re-add only on the owner's instruction. |
| #floatingEstimator + files.withcherry.com/widgets/widget.js init (every page) | REPLACE | The site-wide floating Cherry estimator is a third-party script on every page; it is not reproduced. Its function stays as a local link to /cherry-payment-plan/ (that page: Cherry mode "link"). Decision CY-1, docs/OPEN-DECISIONS.md. |
| /cherry-payment-plan/ div.ecp-html (the Cherry widget: fonts link, loader, init, mount points) | REPLACE | The widget does not work standalone (tested from a non-practice origin: only its static hero draws, its gql.withcherry.com API calls fail, it throws, and it loads Segment analytics). Per decision CY-1 the page shows a link instead; the source markup holds no Cherry application URL, so the page's own label renders with data-needs="cherry application url" until the practice supplies one (RFEC_CHERRY_URL). RFEC_CHERRY=embed restores the exact source embed. The widget's text is drawn by Cherry's script and is not in the source HTML. |
| li.gfield--type-captcha (forms 9 and 10) | REMOVE | Invisible Google reCAPTCHA (data-size="invisible") bound to the platform account's site key: platform plumbing; never rendered as a visible "CAPTCHA" field. The forms are inert (data-needs-backend) until an endpoint with its own spam protection is wired. |
| GF honeypot li.gform_validation_container + Akismet block | REMOVE | Anti-spam plumbing of the WordPress form backend (2 pages); the honeypot labels "Email" (form 9) and "Name" (form 10) would pose as real fields. |
| span[itemprop=ratingValue] (display:none) | REMOVE | Hidden schema.org rating value "5"; the visible star count is kept. |
| div.ecp-contactlens-desc > #ecp-contactlens-summary-N + a[href="javascript:void(0)"] toggles | REPLACE | Contact-lens brand pages: each product prints its FULL text once; the truncated excerpt is a duplicate (page-scoped list in `sentences`). The script-only "Read More+" toggle is unwrapped to its words (no link). |
| EyeCarePro-Icons, Foundation Icons, Font Awesome (platform icon fonts) | REMOVE | Platform icon fonts; the scaffold draws no icons. |
| wp-emoji, jQuery, Gravity Forms and Beaver Builder scripts and stylesheets, the commented-out Mercator SSO script, <meta name="admin_url">, body[data-subdomain] | REMOVE | WordPress / platform runtime: no trace of the old platform ships. |
| source JSON-LD of the platform's 7 site-wide types (WebPage, LocalBusiness, MedicalBusiness, Optician, "MedicalSpecialty :: Optometric", Organization, static BreadcrumbList) | REPLACE | Replaced by one valid graph built from the verified facts (Organization + Optometric + the page's own BreadcrumbList); the source used an invalid @type and one static breadcrumb list on every page. The page's own structured data is carried or removed by the rows below. |
| source JSON-LD BlogPosting on 19 page(s) | REPAIR | The post's own BlogPosting is carried into the page graph (src/lib/seo.mjs carryBlogPosting); every key and value is the source's except these repairs: mainEntityOfPage.@id (the platform pointed every post at the home page) becomes the page's canonical; HTML entities inside the headline / description strings are decoded (3 posts); datePublished and dateModified ("February 26, 2023") become ISO 8601 dates, the same date the page prints; the empty image [""] is omitted; publisher.logo.url (the platform CDN copy) becomes the logo file this build ships. The build fails if a headline is not the page h1, a date does not parse, datePublished differs from the printed post date, or the author / publisher is not the practice. Blog posts with no BlogPosting at source get none: /inmode-forma-and-lumecca-a-breakthrough-in-eye-care/, /top-causes-of-dry-eye-in-fort-myers/. |

Structured data describes what the page shows, so it is removed rather than published as page content (per-page why: audit/seo-repairs.json). Open: docs/OPEN-DECISIONS.md B. |
| source JSON-LD VideoObject (unparseable at source) on 3 page(s) | REMOVE | The VideoObject block (the eye-safety YouTube video) does not parse at source - a raw line break inside its description string (JSON.parse: "Bad control character in string literal") - so no search engine read it. ## Imagery

Every photograph of the practice, its doctors and staff, its office and the brands it carries is the practice's own,
downloaded from the live site (`audit/image-inventory.json`, `audit/image-classification.json`). The five staff
portraits the CDN refused (HTTP 403) are shown as honest placeholders with the person's name; no person was generated.
